# 86-Eighty-Six V2 — Character Modeling Crosswalk
## CMR-8 authority routing from reconstruction artifacts back into the canonical V2 corpus

# 0. Function

This document exists to prevent the Character Modeling Reference (CMR) layer from becoming a parallel canon. It answers one question:

> **Where did this reconstruction responsibility come from, and where should a later model go when it needs more precision than the CMR profile, matrix, or diagnostic index provides?**

CMR is a reconstruction front end. It reorganizes already source-grounded evidence around voice, behavior, state change, interlocutor, ordinary life, embodied conduct, and bounded prediction. It does **not** outrank the existing V2 evidence, ledger, specialist, or source-language infrastructure.

The authority order remains:

> **Japanese primary source → Phase-5/Phase-8 source and locator authority → V01–V14 / Alter.1 deep readings → L01–L11 longitudinal ledgers → Documents 01–14 and 16–17 → canonical Document 18 → CMR profiles / matrix / diagnostic index / this crosswalk.**

For exact Japanese wording, source identity, locator state, or evidence classification, always escalate leftward in that chain. For a broad literary interpretation, use the narrowest specialist home rather than treating a profile summary as the final argument.

# I. CMR-8 completion state

- Profiles routed: **16/16**.
- Top-level profile sections routed: **341/341**.
- Directed matrix state rows routed: **318/318**, with **318 unique row IDs**.
- CMR-7 roster-direction adjudications preserved: **240/240**.
- Diagnostic-index rows covered by the source-routing protocol: **2372/2372**.
- Diagnostic index remains **2,372 rows / 21 columns**; CMR-8 does not mutate it.
- The CMR-7 matrix remains **318 unique state rows**; CMR-8 does not mutate it.
- The sole CMR-7 `OPEN_UNDERDETERMINED` roster direction remains **Raiden Shuga → Anju Emma**. It is not filled for crosswalk symmetry.
- Profiles, matrix, diagnostic index, and this crosswalk are `canonical` following CMR-9 QA/promotion.

# II. How to use this crosswalk

Use the narrowest route that matches the question. A later model should not retrieve every upstream document by default.

1. **Need to reproduce voice or wording?** Start at the profile or directed matrix state, then use the CMR diagnostic ID / evidence route, L09, Documents 12 and 16, and finally the exact Japanese source.
2. **Need longitudinal personality change?** Use the profile section as an index, then L01 and the relevant volume deep readings; use the character specialist and Document 14 to bound mature/open interpretation.
3. **Need a relationship?** Use the speaker-side matrix row, then L02 and L09; escalate to Document 04 or Document 10 when the relationship has an established specialist home.
4. **Need institutional, military, body/personhood, grief/ordinary-life, or political interpretation?** Route to the corresponding ledger and specialist document rather than expanding the profile prose.
5. **Need an exact locator?** CMR may point to a Phase-5 ID or `LOCATOR_GAP`. Phase-5 remains canonical locator authority. A `LOCATOR_GAP` retains its exact EPUB route; CMR-8 does not invent missing `VXX-L###` IDs.
6. **Need a prediction or hypothetical scene?** Treat it as `DERIVED_INFERENCE_ONLY`. Synthetic output never becomes evidence and never enters the diagnostic index.

# VI. Directed relationship/register matrix crosswalk

## A. Responsibility split

The matrix owns **speaker-side reconstruction state**: how A addresses, commands, jokes with, protects, resists, or changes register toward B in a specified context. It does not own the full literary meaning of the relationship.

- `86_CHARACTER_RELATIONSHIP_REGISTER_MATRIX.md` — model-facing A→B state.
- `L02_RELATIONSHIP_STATE_LEDGER.md` — canonical longitudinal relationship state.
- `L09_JAPANESE_VOICE_ADDRESS_AND_TERMINOLOGY_LEDGER.md` — address/register history.
- Documents 04/10/06/07/08/09/11 as applicable — broader relationship, institutional, military, personhood, ordinary-life or continuity interpretation.
- Documents 12/16 + Japanese primary source — exact linguistic verification.
- CMR-7 coverage ledger — whether the opposite direction is independently warranted. **Never infer B→A from A→B.**

## D. Absent-direction authority

CMR-8 preserves CMR-7 absence decisions as positive constraints. An absent row is not a missing fact to be automatically completed. A later model must consult the CMR-7 coverage ledger before creating or inferring a reverse relation.

| CMR-7 status | Count | Meaning for later reconstruction |
|---|---:|---|
| `ABSENT_ADJUDICATED` | 81 | Use the CMR-7 adjudication and its notes; do not infer a direction merely because the reverse exists. |
| `ABSENT_NO_WARRANT` | 39 | Use the CMR-7 adjudication and its notes; do not infer a direction merely because the reverse exists. |
| `ABSENT_OPEN` | 1 | Use the CMR-7 adjudication and its notes; do not infer a direction merely because the reverse exists. |
| `PRESENT` | 119 | Use the CMR-7 adjudication and its notes; do not infer a direction merely because the reverse exists. |

The sole open direction is **Raiden Shuga → Anju Emma**. Its status remains `OPEN_UNDERDETERMINED`: Profile/corpus relation exists, but no distinct speaker-side register is stable enough to add without symmetry inference.

# VII. Diagnostic-index function crosswalk

The 2,372-row diagnostic index is a **semantic retrieval aid**, not a second thematic ledger. Its fields should be read as routing signals. Every row already carries an exact source route, a Phase-5 locator or explicit `LOCATOR_GAP`, an evidence layer, modeling tags, and a reason the scene is diagnostic.

## A. Field-to-authority routing

| Diagnostic field / use | Route when more precision is needed |
|---|---|
| `entry_id`, `profile_scene_no` | CMR-local stable retrieval only; no independent canon authority |
| `character`, `state_tag`, `behavior_features`, `why_diagnostic` | `L01_CHARACTER_DEVELOPMENT_LEDGER.md` + character specialist + relevant deep reading |
| `target_or_relation` | directed matrix + `L02_RELATIONSHIP_STATE_LEDGER.md`; exact address also L09 |
| `voice_features`, `character_japanese`, Japanese anchor | L09 → Documents 12/16 → exact Japanese source |
| `volume_or_source`, `source_class`, `source_route` | deep reading / Alter.1 supplemental reading → Japanese source |
| `canonical_locator_id`, `locator_status` | Phase-5 locator lock; never manufacture a missing canonical locator |
| `evidence_layer` | source interpretation only; narration, dialogue, other-character interpretation and interiority remain distinct evidence types |
| `modeling_evidence_tags` | route to the domain ledger/specialist below; tags do not themselves establish a thesis |
| `confidence`, `notes` | CMR reconstruction confidence; D14 and source boundary control unresolved claims |

## B. Domain-function routing

A diagnostic row may serve several functions at once. CMR-8 deliberately does **not** assign each of the 2,372 rows to a newly invented exclusive ontology; doing so would create another interpretive layer. Use the row's existing fields and tags to choose the relevant route below.

| Function class | When to use it | Narrow canonical route |
|---|---|---|
| `VOICE_ADDRESS` | voice/register/self-reference/address/politeness/idiolect evidence | `L09_JAPANESE_VOICE_ADDRESS_AND_TERMINOLOGY_LEDGER.md`; `12_JAPANESE_VOICE_NARRATION_TERMINOLOGY_AND_TRANSLATION_SENSITIVE_FINDINGS.md`; `16_JAPANESE_PASSAGE_AND_TERMINOLOGY_INDEX.md` |
| `RELATIONSHIP` | target-specific speech, behavior, care, conflict and relational state | `L02_RELATIONSHIP_STATE_LEDGER.md`; `04_SPEARHEAD_RELATIONSHIPS_BELONGING_AND_THE_RIGHT_TO_CHANGE.md`; `10_SHIN_LENA_LOVE_DEPENDENCE_HOME_AND_THE_RIGHT_TO_RETURN.md` |
| `DEVELOPMENT_TRAIT` | baseline behavior, developmental change, repair and stable-state claims | `L01_CHARACTER_DEVELOPMENT_LEDGER.md` |
| `ORDINARY_FUTURE` | food, school, play, rest, hobbies, gifts, civilian/future imagination | `L08_CHILDHOOD_ORDINARY_LIFE_AND_FUTURE_LEDGER.md`; `09_CHILDHOOD_PRIDE_TRAUMA_GRIEF_RECOVERY_AND_ORDINARY_LIFE.md` |
| `GRIEF_MEMORY` | death, grief, guilt, mourning, remembrance and inheritance | `L07_DEATH_MEMORY_GRIEF_AND_INHERITANCE_LEDGER.md`; `09_CHILDHOOD_PRIDE_TRAUMA_GRIEF_RECOVERY_AND_ORDINARY_LIFE.md`; `11_LEGION_MEMORY_IDENTITY_AND_POSTHUMAN_CONTINUITY.md` |

## D. Locator/source controls

- Locator-status distribution: `AUDITED_SUPPLEMENTAL_LOCATOR` = **5**; `LOCATOR_GAP` = **2,074**; `PHASE5_LOCKED` = **292**; `PHASE5_LOCKED_PARTIAL_ROUTE` = **1**.
- Source-class distribution: `ALTER1_SUPPLEMENTAL` = **6**; `AUDITED_SUPPLEMENTAL_JAPANESE` = **157**; `MAINLINE_JAPANESE` = **2,209**.
- CMR-8 does not reopen Phase 5 to cosmetically eliminate `LOCATOR_GAP`.
- Alter.1 remains admissible only in its audited supplemental role; chronology-bending or explicitly paratextual material already excluded by the profile passes remains excluded.
- No synthetic reconstruction, generated Japanese, crossover dialogue, or hypothetical scene may enter this index or be used to close an authority gap.

# VIII. Domain escalation recipes

These are operational retrieval recipes for later model use. They are deliberately short and route to existing topical homes instead of generating new synthesis.

| Question type | Recommended route |
|---|---|
| “How does this character normally behave?” | profile → diagnostic index → L01 → character specialist → deep reading/source |
| “How does A speak to B?” | profile relationship section → matrix A→B row → L02 + L09 → D12/D16 → source |
| “Has this relationship changed?” | matrix state rows by time → L02 → relevant deep readings → D04/D10 or other specialist |
| “What exact Japanese supports this voice claim?” | diagnostic ID → canonical locator / EPUB route → L09 → D12/D16 → source |
| “Is this ordinary-life behavior really canonical?” | profile ordinary-life section → L08 + D09 → mainline scene or audited Alter.1 route |
| “How should injury/disability affect reconstruction?” | profile body section → L06 + D08 → relevant deep reading/source; do not infer cure or global incapacity |
| “How does military competence affect personality?” | profile behavior/professional section → L04 + D07 → source; military function is not exhaustive identity |
| “What does this artificial subject count as?” | profile → D08 + D11 (+ L05) → D12/16 for language → source; do not collapse standing, identity, memory, body and humanhood |
| “What does the character believe politically?” | profile behavioral values → L03/L05 as applicable → D05/D06 → D14 for bounded comparison |
| “Is this prediction canon?” | No. Use profile prediction section only as `DERIVED_INFERENCE_ONLY`, then inspect diagnostic and matrix evidence. |
| “Which source wins if artifacts disagree?” | Japanese source → Phase-5/8 → deep reading/ledger → specialist → Document 18 → CMR. |

# IX. Crosswalk-specific non-authority rules

1. **This document routes; it does not adjudicate new character claims.** If a contradiction appears while using it, return to the narrower upstream authority.
2. **Document 18 is not the default evidence destination.** It is the integrated capstone. A character/body/military/relationship/language dispute should stop at the narrower specialist when possible.
3. **The CMR matrix is directional.** A→B is never proof of B→A.
4. **The diagnostic index is not an exhaustive quotation warehouse.** Missing a diagnostic row does not prove absence from the novels.
5. **`LOCATOR_GAP` is transparent incompleteness, not a defect to hide.** Exact EPUB routes remain usable and must not be converted into invented Phase-5 IDs.
6. **Alter.1 is supplemental, not equal to mainline chronology.** Use it for ordinary-life/control evidence only where the audited profile/source rules permit.
7. **No V15+ assumptions.** CMR-8 inherits the V14 source boundary and all 32 final-arc OPEN questions.
8. **Synthetic material is never evidence.** Generated reactions, dialogue or Japanese cannot flow backward into profile, matrix, index, ledger or specialist authority.

# X. CMR-9 gate requirements and completion

CMR-8 established the following preconditions for promotion:

- all sixteen profile files are represented;
- every numbered top-level profile section has a crosswalk row;
- every matrix state row is covered by an upstream relationship/register route;
- all 240 CMR-7 roster-direction adjudications remain discoverable and unchanged;
- every diagnostic row retains a source-first route and can be escalated by function;
- the sole `OPEN_UNDERDETERMINED` direction remains open;
- and crosswalk completion alone does not establish canonical authority.

CMR-9 then tested source grounding, state-versus-trait discipline, relationship specificity, V1 regrounding, the synthetic-example firewall, thematic distinctiveness, reconstruction quality, retrieval quality, and strongest-counterexample resistance. The corpus-wide verdict was `CMR9_PASS_CANONICAL_PROMOTION`.

# XI. CMR-9 canonical promotion and current handoff

CMR-9 promoted the sixteen profiles, the diagnostic index, the 318-row directed matrix, and this crosswalk to `canonical` on 2026-08-19. The promotion preserves the source hierarchy and does not make CMR a parallel canon. The sole `OPEN_UNDERDETERMINED` roster direction—Raiden Shuga → Anju Emma—remains open. Exact wording, source facts and locator authority continue to escalate to Japanese primary sources and Phase-5/8 controls.

**CMR-10 release state:** integrated into frozen boundary release `86-V2-V01-V14-1.0`.

# XII. CMR-10 release integration

CMR-10 integrated this crosswalk into frozen boundary release `86-V2-V01-V14-1.0` on 2026-08-20. Profile and matrix hashes were refreshed after administrative release-metadata updates. No section route, specialist authority, diagnostic route, matrix state, source boundary, or uncertainty disposition was changed.
